( 7 )

[.pa.on.p1653.sq14,margin-top=0.29pt]
it,—the old gentleman shook his head, and
in a tone more expressive by half of sorrow
than reproach,—he said his heart all along
foreboded, and he saw it verified in this, and
from a thousand other observations he had
made upon me, That I should neither think
nor act like any other man’s child:—__But__
__alas!__ continued he, shaking his head a second
time, and wiping away a tear which was
trickling down his cheeks, __My Tristram’s__
__misfortunes began nine months before ever he__
__came into the world__.

[.pa.p1637.sq27,margin-top=15.88pt]
—My mother, who was sitting by, look’d
up,—but she knew no more than her backside
what my father meant,—but my uncle, Mr.
__Toby Shandy__, who had been often informed of
the affair,—understood him very well.

[.ct,margin-top=8.1pt]
[.caps]##CHAP##. [.caps]##IV##.

[.ini.p1665.sq1,margin-top=4.33pt]
I Know there are readers in the world, as
well as many other good people in it,
who are no readers at all, who find them{shy}
selves ill at ease, unless they are let into the
whole secret from first to last, of every thing
which concerns you.

[.pa.jl.p1615.sq2,margin-top=18.2pt]
It is in pure compliance with this humour
of theirs, and from a backwardness in my
nature to disappoint any one soul living, that
I have been so very particular already. As

[.pa.on.jl.sq5,margin-top=-0.23pt]
my life and opinions are likely to make some

[.pa.on.p1665.sq20,margin-top=-0.36pt]
noise in the world, and, if I conjecture right,
will take in all ranks, professions, and deno{shy}
minations of men whatever,—be no less read
than the __Pilgrim’s Progress__ itself—and in the
end, prove the very thing which __Montaigne__
dreaded his Essays should turn out, that is, a
book for a parlour-window;—I find it ne{shy}
cessary to consult every one a little in his turn;

[.ft,margin-top=-0.06pt]
{empty} [.right]#and#

[.pa.on.p1611.sq10,margin-top=0.5pt]
and therefore must beg pardon for going on a
little farther in the same way: For which
cause, right glad I am, that I have begun
the history of myself in the way I have done;
and that I am able to go on, tracing every
thing in it, as __Horace__ says, ab Ovo.

[.pa.p1651.sq16,margin-top=15.27pt]
__Horace__, I know, does not recommend this
fashion altogether: But that gentleman is
speaking only of an epic poem or a tragedy;—
(I forget which),—besides, if it was not so, I
should beg Mr. __Horace’s__ pardon;—for in
writing what I have set about, I shall confine
myself neither to his rules, nor to any man’s
rules that ever lived,

[.pa.on.p1652.sq11,margin-top=12.23pt]

so far back into these things, I can give no
better advice, than that they skip over the re{shy}
maining part of this chapter; for I declare
before hand, ’tis wrote only for the curious
and inquisitive,

cence himself, amounting in the whole, to

possession of her office, together with all its
__rights, members, and appurtenances whatsoever__.

[.pa.jl.p1692,margin-top=16.37pt]

quis, Earl, Viscount, or Baron, in these

it suits in some degree, I will not part with

[.pa.on.jl.p1674,margin-top=-0.41pt]
story ran like wild-fire—“The parson had

[.pa.on.p1682.sq9,margin-top=-0.91pt]
“ a returning fit of pride which had just
“ seized him; and he was going to be well
“ mounted once again in his life; and if it
“ was so, ’twas plain as the sun at noon{shy}
“ day, he would pocket the expense of the
“ licence, ten times told, the very first year:—

[.pa.p1637,margin-top=-0.07pt]
——So that every body was left to judge
“ what were his views in this act of
“ charity.”

[.pa.jl.p1665,margin-top=9.69pt]
What were his views in this, and in every

[.pa.on,margin-top=0.77pt]
other action of his life,—or rather what

[.ft,margin-top=-0.64pt]
[.w21]##{nbsp}##[.caps]##VOL##. I.[.w100]##{nbsp}##D [.right]#were#

// tag::page-44[]
[.verso%notitle]
== Page 44

image::blank.svg[role=spacer,pdfwidth=1pt,pdfheight=0.01pt]

[.hd,margin-top=8.48pt]
( 44 )

[.pa.on.p1642.sq11,margin-top=3.06pt]
were the opinions which floated in the brains
of other people concerning it, was a thought
which too much floated in his own, and too
often broke in upon his rest, when he should
have been sound asleep.

[.pa.p1651,margin-top=15.54pt]
About ten years ago this gentleman had
the good fortune to be made entirely easy
upon that score,—it being just so long since
he left his parish,—and the whole world at
the same time behind him,—and stands ac{shy}
countable to a Judge of whom he will have
no cause to complain.

[.pa.p1667,margin-top=15.58pt]
But there is a fatality attends the actions
of some men: Order them as they will, they
pass thro’ a certain medium, which so twists
and refracts them from their true directions
——that, with all the titles to praise which
a rectitude of heart can give, the doers of
them are nevertheless forced to live and die
without it.

[.ft,margin-top=-5.44pt]
{empty} [.right]#Of#

// tag::page-45[]
[.recto%notitle]
== Page 45

image::blank.svg[role=spacer,pdfwidth=1pt,pdfheight=0.01pt]

[.hd,margin-top=8.48pt]
( 45 )

[.pa.jl.p1623,margin-top=-0.14pt]
Of the truth of which, this gentle man was
a painful example.——But to know by what
means this came to pass,—and to make that

[.pa.on.jl,margin-top=-1.44pt]
knowledge of use to you, I insist upon it

[.pa.on.p1674,margin-top=-0.9pt]
that you read the two following chapters,
which contain such a sketch of his life and
conversation, as will carry its moral along
with it.—When this is done, if nothing stops
us in our way, we will go on with the
midwife.

[.ct,margin-top=21.51pt]
[.caps]##CHAP##. [.caps]##XI##.

[.ini.jl.p1582,margin-top=7.46pt]
YORICK was the parson’s name, and
what is very remarkable in it, (as ap-

[.pa.on.jl,margin-top=-3.46pt]
pears from a most ancient account of the fa-

[.pa.on.p1624,margin-top=-0.38pt]
mily, wrote upon strong vellum, and now in
perfect preservation) it had been exactly so
spelt for near,——I was within an ace of
saying nine hundred years;——but I would
not shake my credit in telling an improba{shy}
ble truth, however indisputable in itself;——

[.ft,margin-top=-0.56pt]
[.w182]##{nbsp}##D 2 [.right]#and#

// end::page-45[]
// page 46
// tag::page-46[]

== Page 46

[.hd,margin-top=8.48pt]
( 46 )

[.pa.on.jl.p1669.sq13,margin-top=1.58pt]
and therefore I shall content myself with only
saying——It had been exactly so spelt, with{shy}
out the least variation or transposition of a
single letter, for I do not know how long;
which is more than I would venture to say
of one half of the best surnames in the king{shy}
dom; which, in a course of years, have gene-

[.pa.on.jl.sq23,margin-top=1.0pt]
rally undergone as many chops and changes as

[.pa.on.p1643.sq2,margin-top=0.34pt]
their owners.—Has this been owing to the
pride, or to the shame of the respective pro{shy}
prietors?—In honest truth, I think, some{shy}
times to the one, and sometimes to the
other, just as the temptation has wrought.
But a villainous affair it is, and will one day
so blend and confound us altogether, that
no one shall be able to stand up and swear,
“That his own great grandfather was the
“ man who did either this or that.”

[.pa.on.jl.p1651,margin-top=21.72pt]
This evil had been sufficiently fenced a-

[.pa.on.sq3,margin-top=-0.25pt]
gainst by the prudent care of the __Yorick’s__ fa-

[.ft,margin-top=0.7pt]
{empty} [.right]#mily,#

( 47 )

[.pa.on.jl.p1629.sq10,margin-top=2.04pt]
mily, and their religious preservation of these
records I quote, which do farther inform us,
That the family was originally of __Danish__
extraction, and had been transplanted into
__England__ as early as in the reign of __Horwen-__

[.pa.on.jl,margin-top=-1.18pt]
__dillus__, king of __Denmark__, in whose court, it

[.pa.on.p1651.sq16,margin-top=0.29pt]
seems, an ancestor of this Mr. __Yorick’s__, and
from whom he was lineally descended, held
a considerable post to the day of his death.
Of what nature this considerable post was,
this record saith not;—it only adds, That,
for near two centuries, it had been totally
abolished, as altogether unnecessary, not only
in that court, but in every other court of
the Christian world.

[.pa.jl.p1662,margin-top=14.23pt]
It has often come into my head, that this

[.pa.on.p1664.sq5,margin-top=-0.3pt]
post could be no other than that of the king’s
chief Jester;—and that __Hamlet__ ’s __Yorick__, in
our __Shakespeare__, many of whose plays, you
know, are founded upon authenticated facts,
was certainly the very man.

[.ft,margin-top=-0.81pt]

I have not the time to look into __Saxo-{zwsp}__

and can easily get at the book, you may do
it full as well yourself. I

[.pa.p1651.sq17,margin-top=21.03pt]
had just time, in my travels through

in the year 1741, I accompanied as gover{shy}
nor, riding along with him at a prodigious
rate thro’ most parts of __Europe__, and of which
original journey performed by us two, a most
delectable narrative will be given in the pro{shy}
gress of this work, I had just time, I say,
and that was all, to prove the truth of an
observation made by a long sojourner in that
country;——namely, “That nature was
“ neither very lavish, nor was she very stin{shy}
“ gy in her gifts of genius and capacity to
“ its inhabitants;—but, like a discreet pa{shy}
“ rent, was moderately kind to them all;

[.ft,margin-top=-0.3pt]
{empty} [.right]#“ observing#

“ observing such an equal tenor in the dis{shy}

ter degree in this unsettled island, where na{shy}
ture, in her gifts and dispositions of this
kind, is most whimsical and capricious; for

This is all that ever staggered my faith in

I can remember of him, and by all the ac{shy}
counts I could ever get of him, seemed not to
have had one single drop of __Danish__ blood in
his whole crasis; in nine hundred years, it
might possibly have all run out:——I will not
philosophize one moment with you about it;
for happen how it would, the fact was this:—
That instead of that cold phlegm and
exact regularity of sense and humours, you
would have looked for, in one so extracted;
—he was, on the contrary, as mercurial and
sublimated a composition,—as heteroclite
a creature in all his declensions;—with as
much life and whim, and __gaité de cœur__ about
him, as the kindliest climate could have en{shy}
gendered and put together. With all this

vity as such;—for where gravity was want{shy}

but he was an enemy to the affectation of it,
and declared open war against it, only as it

[.pa.jl.p1670.sq2,margin-top=-0.62pt]
In the naked temper which a merry heart

ger,——but to itself;——whereas the very

quently deceit;——’twas a taught trick to

[.pa.on.p1678,margin-top=0.16pt]
gain credit of the world for more sense and

But, in plain truth, he was a man un-

[.pa.on.jl.p1664.sq15,margin-top=-0.59pt]

tion was made of a pitiful or an ungene-

rous proceeding——he never gave himself a

moment’s time to reflect who was the hero

[.caps]##CHAP##. [.caps]##XII##.

[.ini.jl.p1637,margin-top=8.82pt]
THE __Mortgager__ and __Mortgagée__ differ the
one from the other, not more in length

[.pa.on.jl,margin-top=-4.19pt]
of purse, than the __Jester__ and __Jestée__ do, in

[.pa.on.jl.p1697,margin-top=-0.21pt]
that of memory. But in this the comparison

[.pa.on.jl,margin-top=-0.48pt]
between them runs, as the scholiasts call it,

[.pa.on.jl.p1706,margin-top=-1.07pt]
upon all-four; which, by the by, is upon

[.pa.on.jl.p1730.sq16,margin-top=-1.35pt]
one or two legs more than some of the best of

[.pa.on.jl.p1746,margin-top=-0.47pt]
__Homer’s__ can pretend to;—namely, That
the one raises a sum, and the other a laugh

[.pa.on.jl,margin-top=0.37pt]
at your expense, and thinks no more about

[.pa.on.jl.p1696,margin-top=0.62pt]
it. Interest, however, still runs on in both

[.pa.on.jl.p1746,margin-top=-0.31pt]
cases;—the periodical or accidental pay-

[.pa.on.jl,margin-top=0.37pt]
ments of it, just serving to keep the memory

[.pa.on.jl.p1698,margin-top=0.34pt]
of the affair alive; till, at length, in some

[.pa.on.jl,margin-top=-0.76pt]
evil hour,—pop comes the creditor upon

[.pa.on.p1738,margin-top=-0.68pt]
each, and by demanding principal upon the
spot, together with full interest to the very
day, makes them both feel the full extent of
their obligations,

[.ft,margin-top=-6.72pt]
{empty} [.right]#As#

As the reader (for I hate your __ifs__) has a
thorough knowledge of human nature, I
need not say more to satisfy him, that my
[.sc]##Hero## could not go on at this rate without
some slight experience of these incidental
mementos. To speak the truth, he had
wantonly involved himself in a multitude of
small book-debts of this stamp, which, not{shy}
withstanding __Eugenius’s__ frequent advice, he
too much disregarded; thinking, that as
not one of them was contracted thro’ any
malignancy;—but, on the contrary, from
an honesty of mind, and a mere jocundity
of humour, they would all of them be cross’d
out in course.

[.pa.p1659.sq13,margin-top=20.87pt]
__Eugenius__ would never admit this; and would
often tell him, that one day or other he would
certainly be reckoned with; and he would
often add, in an accent of sorrowful appre{shy}
hension,——to the uttermost mite. To

[.ft,margin-top=-0.62pt]

at it these six weeks, making all the speed I
possibly could,—and am not yet born:—I
have just been able, and that’s all, to tell
you __when__ it happen’d, but not __how__;—so that
you see the thing is yet far from being accom{shy}
plished.

[.pa.on,margin-top=-0.45pt]
was this:—“That in case my mother here-

“ after should, at any time, put my father

“ that for every such instance, she should for-

[.pa.on.jl.p1664,margin-top=-0.04pt]

“ to no more,—and so on, __toties quoties__, in

[.pa.on,margin-top=0.24pt]
“ nant betwixt them had not been made.”

[.pa.p1653,margin-top=0.01pt]
——This, by the way, was no more than
what was reasonable;—and yet, as reasona{shy}
ble as it was, I have ever thought it hard
that the whole weight of the article should
have fallen entirely, as it did, upon myself.

[.pa.p1649,margin-top=18.59pt]
But I was begot and born to misfortunes;
—for my own poor mother, whether it was
wind or water,—or a compound of both,—
or neither;—or whether it was simply the
mere swell of imagination and fancy in her;
——or how far a strong wish and desire to

[.ft,margin-top=0.52pt]
{empty} [.right]#have#

// tag::page-84[]

[.hd,margin-top=8.48pt]
( 84 )

[.pa.on.jl.p1678,margin-top=2.61pt]
have it so, might mislead her judgment;—
in short, whether she was deceived or de{shy}
ceiving in this matter, it no way becomes
me to decide. The fact was this, That in
the latter end of __September__ 1717, which was

[.pa.on.jl.p1724,margin-top=-0.1pt]
the year before I was born, my mother hav-

[.pa.on.jl.p1665,margin-top=0.48pt]
ing carried my father up to town much a{shy}
gainst the grain,—he peremptorily insisted

[.pa.on.jl,margin-top=1.08pt]
upon the clause;—so that I was doom’d, by

[.pa.on.p1678,margin-top=0.7pt]
marriage articles, to have my nose squeez’d
as flat to my face, as if the destinies had ac{shy}
tually spun me without one.

[.pa.p1667.sq2,margin-top=18.74pt]
How this event came about,—and what a
train of vexatious disappointments, in one
stage or other of my life, have pursued me
from the mere loss, or rather compression,
of this one single member,—shall be laid be{shy}
fore the reader all in due time.

[.ft,margin-top=37.46pt]
{empty} [.right]#CHAP.#

// page 85
// tag::page-85[]
[.recto%notitle]
== Page 85

image::blank.svg[role=spacer,pdfwidth=1pt,pdfheight=0.01pt]

[.hd,margin-top=8.48pt]
( 85 )

[.ct,margin-top=34.83pt]
[.caps]##CHAP##. [.caps]##XVI##.

[.ini.jl.p1501,margin-top=10.05pt]
MY father, as any body may naturally
imagine, came down with my mo-

[.pa.on.jl,margin-top=-3.19pt]
ther into the country, in but a pettish kind

[.pa.on.jl.p1666,margin-top=0.19pt]
of a humour. The first twenty or five-and-{zwsp}

[.pa.on.jl.p1667,margin-top=-0.29pt]
twenty miles he did nothing in the world
but fret and teaze himself, and indeed my
mother too, about the cursed expense,
which he said might every shilling of it have
been saved;—then what vexed him more
than every thing else was, the provoking
time of the year,—which, as I told you,

[.pa.on.p1665,margin-top=-0.16pt]
was towards the end of __September__, when his
wall-fruit, and green gages especially, in
which he was very curious, where just rea{shy}
dy for pulling:——“Had he been whistled up
“ to __London__, upon a __Tom Fool’s__ errand, in
“ any other month of the whole year, he
“ should not have said three words about it.”

[.ft,margin-top=-1.45pt]
{empty} [.right]#For#

// end::page-85[]
// page 86
// tag::page-86[]
[.verso%notitle]
== Page 86

image::blank.svg[role=spacer,pdfwidth=1pt,pdfheight=0.01pt]

[.hd,margin-top=8.48pt]
( 86 )

[.pa.jl.p1637,margin-top=1.46pt]
For the next two whole stages, no subject
would go down, but the heavy blow he had
sustain’d from the loss of a son, whom it

[.pa.on.jl,margin-top=-0.18pt]
seems he had fully reckon’d upon in his

[.pa.on.jl.p1651,margin-top=0.02pt]
mind, and register’d down in his pocket{shy}
book, as a second staff for his old age, in
case __Bobby__ should fail him. The disappoint-

[.pa.on.jl.p1664,margin-top=0.05pt]
“ ment of this, he said, was ten times more

[.pa.on.jl,margin-top=0.51pt]
“ to a wise man, than all the money which

[.pa.on.p1664,margin-top=1.04pt]
“ the journey, &c. had cost him, put to{shy}
“ gether,—rot the hundred and twenty
“ pounds,——he did not mind it a rush.

[.pa.p1660.sq29,margin-top=21.04pt]
From __Stilton__, all the way to __Grantham__,
nothing in the whole affair provoked him so
much as the condolences of his friends, and
the foolish figure they should both make at
church, the first __Sunday__;——of which, in the
satirical vehemence of his wit, now shar{shy}
pen’d a little by vexation, he would give so
many humorous and provoking descriptions,—

[.ft,margin-top=-0.27pt]
{empty} [.right]#and#

// end::page-86[]
// page 87
// tag::page-87[]
[.recto%notitle]
== Page 87

image::blank.svg[role=spacer,pdfwidth=1pt,pdfheight=0.01pt]

[.hd,margin-top=8.48pt]
( 87 )

[.pa.on.p1645,margin-top=-3.49pt]
and place his rib and self in so many tor{shy}
menting lights and attitudes in the face of
the whole congregation;—that my mother
declared, these two stages where so truly
tragicomical, that she did nothing but
laugh and cry in a breath, from one end to
the other of them all the way.

[.pa.jl.p1678,margin-top=18.34pt]
From __Grantham__, till they had cross’d the
__Trent__, my father was out of all kind of pati{shy}
ence at the vile trick and imposition which

[.pa.on.jl.p1692,margin-top=-1.03pt]
he fancied my mother had put upon him in
this affair—“Certainly,” he would say to

[.pa.on.jl.p1692,margin-top=-0.26pt]
himself, over and over again, “the woman
could not be deceived herself——if she could,

[.pa.on.jl.p1713,margin-top=-0.65pt]
——what weakness!”—tormenting word!—
which led his imagination a thorny dance,
and, before all was over, play’d the duce
and all with him;——for sure as ever the word

[.pa.on.p1692,margin-top=0.33pt]
__weakness__ was uttered, and stuck full upon
his brain—so sure it set him upon running

[.ft,margin-top=0.09pt]
{empty} [.right]#divisions#

// end::page-87[]
// page 88
// tag::page-88[]
[.verso%notitle]
== Page 88

image::blank.svg[role=spacer,pdfwidth=1pt,pdfheight=0.01pt]

[.hd,margin-top=8.48pt]
( 88 )

[.pa.on.p1693.sq24,margin-top=1.45pt]
divisions upon how many kinds of weaknesses
there were;——that there was such a thing
as weakness of the body,——as well as weak{shy}
ness of the mind,—and then he would do
nothing but syllogize within himself for a
stage or two together, How far the cause of
these vexations might, or might not, have
arisen out of himself.

[.pa.jl.p1697,margin-top=17.6pt]
In short, he had so many little subjects
of disquietude springing out of this one
affair, all fretting successively in his mind as
they rose up in it, that my mother, what-

[.pa.on.jl.p1760,margin-top=-0.87pt]
ever was her journey up, had but an uneasy
journey of it down.——In a word, as she com{shy}
plained to my uncle __Toby__, he would have

[.pa.on,margin-top=0.35pt]
tired out the patience of any flesh alive.

[.ft,margin-top=41.65pt]
{empty} [.right]#CHAP.#

// end::page-88[]
// page 89
// tag::page-89[]
[.recto%notitle]
== Page 89

image::blank.svg[role=spacer,pdfwidth=1pt,pdfheight=0.01pt]

[.hd,margin-top=8.48pt]
( 89 )

[.ct,margin-top=23.65pt]
[.caps]##CHAP##. [.caps]##XVII##.

[.ini.jl.p1637.sq9,margin-top=14.55pt]
THough my father travelled homewards,
as I told you, in none of the best of
moods,—pshawing and pishing all the way
down,—yet he had the complaisance to keep

[.pa.on.jl,margin-top=0.64pt]
the worst part of the story still to himself;—

[.pa.on.jl.sq11,margin-top=-0.64pt]
which was the resolution he had taken of do-

[.pa.on.jl.p1689.sq17,margin-top=-0.44pt]
ing himself the justice, which my uncle __Toby’s__
clause in the marriage settlement empowered
him; nor was it till the very night in which
I was begot, which was thirteen months
after, that she had the least intimation of
his design: when my father, happening,
as you remember, to be a little chagrin’d

[.pa.on.p1713,margin-top=-1.03pt]
and out of temper,——took occasion as they
lay chatting gravely in bed afterwards, talk{shy}
ing over what was to come,——to let her
know that she must accommodate herself as
well as she could to the bargain made be-

[.ft,margin-top=-1.69pt]
{empty} [.right]#tween#

// end::page-89[]
// page 90
// tag::page-90[]
[.verso%notitle]
== Page 90

image::blank.svg[role=spacer,pdfwidth=1pt,pdfheight=0.01pt]

[.hd,margin-top=8.48pt]
( 90 )

[.pa.on.p1651,margin-top=3.02pt]
tween them in their marriage deeds; which
was to lye-in of her next child in the coun{shy}
try, to balance the last year’s journey.

[.pa.p1664,margin-top=20.25pt]
My father was a gentleman of many virt{shy}
ues,—but he had a strong spice of that
in his temper, which might, or might not,
add to the number.—’Tis known by the
name of perseverance in a good cause,—
and of obstinacy in a bad one: Of this my
mother had so much knowledge, that she
knew ’twas to no purpose to make any re{shy}
monstrance,—so she e’en resolved to sit
down quietly, and make the most of it.

There was little danger, he would say, of

feared, that in some violent push, we should
go off, all at once, in a state-apoplexy;—
and then he would say, __The Lord have mercy__
__upon us all__.

[.pa.on.p1634.sq15,margin-top=-0.84pt]
tution was hourly establishing,—would, in the
end, prove fatal to the monarchical system
of domestick government established in the
first creation of things by God.

__Jenny__! tender as the appellation is, may be
my child.——Consider,—I was born in the
year eighteen.—Nor is there any thing
unnatural or extravagant in the supposition,
that my dear __Jenny__ may be my friend.—

which thing is not likely to be found out;—
I think it is full as abominable, that a man

the world with the conceit of it rotting in
his head.

[.pa,margin-top=14.54pt]
This is precisely my situation.

[.pa.jl.p1637,margin-top=15.64pt]

accidentally led into, as in all my digressions

[.pa.on.jl,margin-top=-0.73pt]

[.pa.on.p1692.sq26,margin-top=-0.18pt]
all along, I fear, been overlooked by my rea{shy}
der,—not for want of penetration in him,

sions are all fair, as you observe,—and that
I fly off from what I am about, as far, and as

[.ft,margin-top=-0.48pt]

By this contrivance the machinery of my
work is of a species by itself; two contrary
motions are introduced into it, and recon{shy}
ciled, which are thought to be at variance
with each other. In a word, my work is
digressive, and it is progressive too,—and at
the same time.

[.pa.jl.p1658,margin-top=13.51pt]
This, Sir, is a very different story from

[.pa.on.jl.p1637.sq4,margin-top=1.53pt]
that of the earth’s moving round her axis, in
her diurnal rotation, with her progress in her
elliptick orbit which brings about the year,

[.pa.on.jl.sq8,margin-top=-0.72pt]
and constitutes that variety and vicissitude of

[.pa.on.jl.p1637,margin-top=-0.19pt]
seasons we enjoy;—though I own it suggest{shy}
ed the thought,—as I believe the greatest of

[.pa.on.p1637,margin-top=1.64pt]
our boasted improvements and discoveries
have come from some such trifling hints.

[.pa.jl.p1694,margin-top=13.62pt]
Digressions, incontestably, are the sun-

[.pa.on.jl.p1705,margin-top=-0.07pt]
shine;——they are the life, the soul of read-

[.pa.on.jl.p1637,margin-top=-0.34pt]
ing!—take them out of this book, for in-

[.pa.on,margin-top=1.18pt]
stance,—you might as well take the book

[.pa.p1659,margin-top=18.89pt]
All the dexterity is in the good cookery

he goes on with his main work,—then there
is an end of his digression.

[.pa.on.jl.p1637,margin-top=0.37pt]

But this, as I said above, is not the case
of the inhabitants of this earth;—our minds
shine not through the body, but are wrapt
up here in a dark covering of uncrystalized
flesh and blood; so that, if we would come
to the specific characters of them, we must
go some other way to work.

[.pa.on.p1651,margin-top=18.61pt]
Many, in good truth, are the ways, which
human wit has been forced to take, to do
this thing with exactness.

[.pa.p1654.sq6,margin-top=18.66pt]
Some, for instance, draw all their charac{shy}
ters with wind instruments.— __Virgil__ takes
notice of that way in the affair of __Dido__ and
__Aeneas__;—but it is as fallacious as the breath
of fame;—and, moreover, bespeaks a
narrow genius. I am not ignorant that the
__Italians__ pretend to a mathematical exactness
in their designations of one particular sort of
character among them, from the __forte__ or

It is now high time, however, that I give you

[.ct,margin-top=17.04pt]
[.caps]##CHAP##. [.caps]##XXV##.

[.ini.jl.p1637,margin-top=9.91pt]
THE wounds in my uncle __Toby’s__ groin,
which he received at the siege of __Na{shy}__
__mur__, rendering him unfit for the service, it

[.pa.on.p1773,margin-top=-0.13pt]
was thought expedient he should return to
__England__, in order, if possible, to be set to
rights.

[.pa.jl.p1641,margin-top=12.4pt]
He was four years totally confined,—part

[.pa.on.jl,margin-top=-0.74pt]
of it to his bed, and all of it to his room;

[.pa.on.p1653.sq29,margin-top=-0.27pt]
and in the course of his cure, which was all
that time in hand, suffer’d unspeakable mise{shy}
ries,—owing to a succession of exfoliations
from the __os pubis__, and the outward edge of that
part of the __coxendiz__ called the __os illeum__,——

[.ft,margin-top=-0.35pt]
{empty} [.right]#both#

both which bones were dismally crush’d, as
much by the irregularity of the stone, which
I told you was broke off the parapet,—as by
its size,—(tho’ it was pretty large) which in{shy}
clined the surgeon all along to think, that
the great injury which it had done my uncle

[.pa.on.jl.p1691,margin-top=-0.18pt]
__Toby’s__ groin, was more owing to the gravity

[.pa.on.jl.sq9,margin-top=-1.54pt]
of the stone itself, than to the projectile force

[.pa.on.p1691,margin-top=-0.99pt]

[.pa.jl.p1643,margin-top=14.98pt]
My father at that time was just beginning

[.pa.on.jl.p1691,margin-top=0.52pt]

[.pa.on.jl,margin-top=-0.73pt]

tance to step into the house on any occasion,

[.pa.p1682.sq11,margin-top=13.95pt]
The history of a soldier’s wound beguiles
the pain of it;—my uncle’s visitors at least
thought so, and in their daily calls upon him,
from the courtesy arising out of that belief,
they would frequently turn the discourse to
that subject,—and from that subject the dis{shy}
course would generally roll on to the siege it{shy}
self.

[.pa.jl.p1651.sq9,margin-top=13.97pt]
These conversations where infinitely kind;
and my uncle __Toby__ received great relief from

[.pa.on.p1651.sq16,margin-top=1.41pt]
them, and would have received much more,
but that they brought him into some unfore{shy}
seen perplexities, which, for three months to{shy}
gether, retarded his cure greatly; and if he
had not hit upon an expedient to extricate
himself out of them, I verily believe they
would have laid him in his grave,

[.pa.p1646.sq26,margin-top=3.32pt]
What these perplexities of my uncle __Toby__
were,——’tis impossible for you to guess;—if
you could,—I should blush; not as a relati{shy}
on,—not as a man,—nor even as a woman,
—but I should blush as an author; inasmuch
as I set no small store by myself upon this
very account, that my reader has never yet
been able to guess at any thing. And in this,
Sir, I am of so nice and singular a humour,
that if I thought you was able to form the
least judgment or probable conjecture to your{shy}
self, of what was to come in the next page,
—I would tear it out of my book.

[.ct,margin-top=20.79pt]
[.caps]##CHAP##. [.caps]##XXVI##.

[.ini.jl.p1528,margin-top=9.91pt]
I Have begun a new book, on purpose that
I might have room enough to explain the

[.pa.on.jl,margin-top=-4.1pt]
nature of the perplexities in which my uncle

[.pa.on.jl.p1664.sq15,margin-top=0.24pt]

[.pa.on.jl,margin-top=0.63pt]
themselves upon the counter-guard,—and

withstanding the gallantry of the __French__ offi-

cis sword in hand,

the abbey of __Salsines__, &c., and give his visi{shy}
tors as distinct a history of each of their at{shy}
tacks, as of that of the gate of __St. Nicolas__,
where he had the honour to receive his
wound.

[.pa.jl.p1689.sq9,margin-top=12.47pt]
But the desire of knowledge, like the thirst of
riches, increases ever with the acquisition of
it. The more my uncle __Toby__ pored over his
map, the more he took a liking to it!—by

[.pa.on.jl.p1662,margin-top=0.23pt]
the same process and electrical assimilation,

[.pa.on.jl.p1692,margin-top=-0.95pt]
as I told you, thro’ which I ween the souls
of connoisseurs themselves, by long friction

[.pa.on.p1692,margin-top=-0.55pt]
and incumbition, have the happiness, at
length, to get all be-virtued,—be-pictured,
—be-butterflied, and befiddled.

[.pa.jl.p1692.sq14,margin-top=14.71pt]
The more my uncle __Toby__ drank of this
sweet fountain of science, the greater was the

[.pa.on,margin-top=0.91pt]
heat and impatience of his thirst, so that be-

[.ft,margin-top=-0.08pt]
{empty} [.right]#fore#

you had no hold of him—he was voluble;

[.pa.on.jl.p1719,margin-top=-1.5pt]

[.pa.on.jl,margin-top=1.86pt]
with the respectfulness of Corporal __Trim’s__

[.hd,margin-top=8.48pt]
( 226 )

[.pa.p1626,margin-top=1.51pt]
’Tis his turn now;—I have given an am{shy}
ple description of Dr. __Slop’s__ sad overthrow,
and of his sad appearance in the back-par{shy}
lour;—his imagination must now go on with
it for a while.

[.pa.jl.p1638.sq12,margin-top=14.66pt]
Let the reader imagine then, that Dr.
__Slop__ has told his tale—and in what words,
and with what aggravations, his fancy choo{shy}
ses;—Let him suppose, that __Obadiah__ has
told his tale also, and with such rueful looks
of affected concern, as he thinks will best
contrast the two figures as they stand by each
other.——Let him imagine, that my father
has stepped up stairs to see my mother.—
And, to conclude this work of imagination

[.pa.on.jl,margin-top=-1.08pt]
—let him imagine the doctor washed,—rub-

[.pa.on.p1705,margin-top=-0.25pt]
bed down, and condoled,—felicitated,
—got into a pair of __Obadiah’s__ pumps, step{shy}
ping forwards towards the door, upon the
very point of entering upon action.

[.ft,margin-top=6.72pt]
{empty} [.right]#Truce!#

// end::page-226[]
// page 227

[.recto%notitle]
== Page 227

image::blank.svg[role=spacer,pdfwidth=1pt,pdfheight=0.01pt]

[.hd,margin-top=8.48pt]
( 227 )

[.pa.jl.p1667,margin-top=2.13pt]
Truce!—truce, good Dr. __Slop__:—stay thy

[.pa.on.jl,margin-top=1.85pt]
obstetric hand;——return it safe into thy

[.pa.on.jl.p1691.sq21,margin-top=0.09pt]
bosom to keep it warm;——little dost thou
know what obstacles,———little dost thou
think what hidden causes, retard its operation!

[.pa.on.jl.p1656,margin-top=0.73pt]
——Hast thou; Dr. __Slop__,—hast thou been

[.pa.on.jl.p1691,margin-top=-1.17pt]
intrusted with the secret articles of this so{shy}
lemn treaty which has brought thee into this

[.pa.on.jl.p1668,margin-top=-0.16pt]
place?—Art thou aware that at this instant,

[.pa.on.jl.p1680.sq11,margin-top=-0.93pt]
a daughter of __Lucina__ is put obstetrically over
thy head? Alas!—’tis too true.—Besides,
great son of __Pilumnus!__ what canst thou do?
—Thou hast come forth unarm’d;—thou
hast left thy __tire-tête__,—thy new-invented __for-

[.pa.on.jl.p1709,margin-top=-1.2pt]
ceps__,—thy __crotchet__,—thy __squirt__, and all thy

[.pa.on.jl.p1691,margin-top=-0.89pt]
instruments of. salvation and deliverance,
behind thee,—By Heaven! at this moment

[.pa.on.jl.p1691,margin-top=0.29pt]
they are hanging up in a green bays bag, be{shy}
twixt thy two pistols, at the bed’s head!—

[.pa.on,margin-top=1.73pt]
Ring;——call;——send——__Obadiah__ back up-

[.ft,margin-top=0.19pt]
